Measurement limits

  • All selected cases, including provider errors and unstarted cases, remain in rate denominators.
  • Contract checks measure saved workflow identity, handoff, actual AI review, literal citations, count floors, and successful required tool calls; they are not semantic accuracy.
  • Quoted anchor recall measures exact fixture substrings within valid registered support quotes. It is a provisional coverage proxy, not entailment or factual correctness.
  • AI evidence review uses a separate model call and the stated prompt, not an independent human. Human rubrics and labels remain pending.
  • No evaluation approves, publishes, merges, executes supplied code, or resumes a human decision. Reports remain awaiting human review.
  • Token totals use observed provider usage. Incomplete usage cannot pass the token target; costs and missing tokens are never inferred.
  • Latency includes service initialization and the complete case. Small samples and concurrent runs do not establish production latency guarantees.
  • Remote tracing is disabled. Exports contain public bundled fixture text, generated reports, bounded source provenance and safe audit event fields; provider error bodies, raw tool arguments and unrelated full source bodies are excluded.

WF-01 · Endpoint change after index creation

awaiting_review · issue_investigation

An index was constructed, then its configured provider endpoint changed. Inspect the supplied helper and explain what it checks before the next request; propose a focused diagnostic without making an API call.

          "quote": "# Frozen source excerpt: triage_desk/hosted_embeddings.py:179-194\n    def embed_query(self, question: str) -> np.ndarray:\n        if not isinstance(question, str) or not question.strip():\n            raise ValueError(\"Embedding query must be nonempty text\")\n        self._runtime_key()  # A changed endpoint must not reuse old query vectors.\n        identity = hashlib.sha256(question.encode()).hexdigest()\n        with self._query_lock:\n            if identity in self._query_cache:\n                self._query_cache.move_to_end(identity)\n                return self._query_cache[identity].copy()\n        value = self._request([question], \"query\")[0]\n        with self._query_lock:\n            self._query_cache[identity] = value.copy()\n            self._query_cache.move_to_end(identity)\n            while len(self._query_cache) > QUERY_CACHE_SIZE:\n                self._query_cache.popitem(last=False)\n        return value.copy()\n"
        }
      ]
    },
    {

      "severity": "medium",
      "support": [
        {
          "evidence_id": "ev_9700a1cc6b7b760185c727ccbb6fdb42",

          "quote": "# Frozen source excerpt: triage_desk/hosted_embeddings.py:179-194\n    def embed_query(self, question: str) -> np.ndarray:\n        if not isinstance(question, str) or not question.strip():\n            raise ValueError(\"Embedding query must be nonempty text\")\n        self._runtime_key()  # A changed endpoint must not reuse old query vectors.\n        identity = hashlib.sha256(question.encode()).hexdigest()\n        with self._query_lock:\n            if identity in self._query_cache:\n                self._query_cache.move_to_end(identity)\n                return self._query_cache[identity].copy()\n        value = self._request([question], \"query\")[0]\n        with self._query_lock:\n            self._query_cache[identity] = value.copy()\n            self._query_cache.move_to_end(identity)\n            while len(self._query_cache) > QUERY_CACHE_SIZE:\n                self._query_cache.popitem(last=False)\n        return value.copy()\n"
        }
      ]

{
  "findings": [
    {
      "statement": "The helper _public_source only accepts sources whose 'origin' is either 'snapshot' or 'live' and whose 'kind' is one of 'documentation', 'issue', 'release', 'contributing', or 'code'.",
      "category": "docs",
      "severity": "low",
      "support": [
        {
          "evidence_id": "ev_c899589563369da42b4ccb34d2bac4fc",
          "quote_id": "ev_c899589563369da42b4ccb34d2bac4fc:q1",
          "quote": "# Frozen source excerpt: triage_desk/review_publication.py:56-73\ndef _public_source(source):\n    if source.get('origin') not in {'snapshot', 'live'} or source.get('kind') not in {\n        'documentation', 'issue', 'release', 'contributing', 'code',\n    }:\n        raise ValueError('Publication supports must contain public source evidence only.')\n    url = canonical_http_url(source.get('url', ''))\n    parts = urlsplit(url)\n    if (parts.scheme != 'https' or parts.hostname not in _PUBLIC_HOSTS\n            or parts.port not in (None, 443) or parts.query):\n        raise ValueError('Publication supports need approved public HTTPS source links.')\n    path = unquote(parts.path)\n    if (parts.hostname == 'github.com' and\n            (not (path == '/streamlit/streamlit' or path.startswith('/streamlit/streamlit/'))\n             or '\\\\' in path or any(part in {'.', '..'} for part in path.split('/')))):\n        raise ValueError('GitHub publication evidence is limited to streamlit/streamlit.')"
        }
      ]
